return X, depths, doc_ids\\n\\ndef get_oof_predictions(X, y, groups):\\n    gkf = GroupKFold(n_splits=5)\\n    oof_proba = np.zeros(len(y))\\n    fold_aucs = []\\n    for train_idx, test_idx in       clf.fit(X[train_idx], y[train_idx])\\n        proba = clf.predict_proba(X[test_idx])[:, 1]\\n        oof_proba[test_idx] = proba\\n        fold_aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[test_idx], proba))\\n d)[0] for d in unique_docs]\\n    \\n    point_auc = roc_auc_score(y, oof_proba)\\n    boot_aucs = np.empty(n_boot)\\n    \\n    for b in range(n_boot):\\n        sampled = rng.randint(0, n_docs, np.unique(doc_ids, return_inverse=True)\\n    n_docs = len(unique_docs)\\n    \\n    count_ge = 0\\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\\n        doc_labels = rng.randint(0, 2, size=n_docs)\\n        perm_y = {(y_pair==0).sum()}, class 1: {(y_pair==1).sum()})\\\", flush=True)\\n        \\n        oof_proba, fold_aucs = get_oof_predictions(X_pair, y_pair, groups_pair)\\n        print(f\\\"  Fold AUCs: {[round(a,4) for a in fold_aucs]}\\\", flush=True)\\n        print(f\\\"  Mean AUC: {np.mean(fold_aucs):.4f}\\\", flush=True)\\n        \\n        point_auc, ci_lower, ci_upper, boot_aucs = bootstrap_ci(\\n            y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, N_BOOT, rng\\n        )\\n        print(f\\\"  Point AUC (OOF): {point_auc:.4f}\\\", flush=True)\\n        print(f\\\"  95% CI: sklearn.model_selection import GroupKFold\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\\n\\nFEAT_COLS = [\\n    \\\"surp_mean\\\", \\\"surp_std\\\", \\\"surp_skew\\\", \\\"surp_kurt\\\",\\n    \\\"surp_d1_mean\\\", \\\"surp_d1_std\\\", \\\"surp_d1_skew\\\",\\n    \\\"surp_d2_mean\\\", \\\"surp_d2_std\\\",\\n    \\\"ttr\\\", \\\"hapax_ratio\\\", \\\"self_bleu\\\",\\n    \\\"freq_kurtosis\\\", \\\"freq_entropy\\\", \\\"low_freq_ratio\\\",\\n]\\n\\nDATA_DIR = Path(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data_exp019_mistral7b\\\")\\nOUT_PATH = Path(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/artifacts/exp019_bootstrap_ci.json\\\")\\nN_BOOT = 10000\\nN_PERM = 10000\\nMAX_DEPTH = 5\\nSEED = 42\\nTHETA = 0.60\\n\\ndef load_data():\\n    with open(DATA_DIR / \\\"features.csv\\\") as f:\\n        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))\\n    depths = np.array([int(r[\\\"depth\\\"]) for r in rows])\\n    doc_ids = np.array([int(r[\\\"doc_id\\\"]) for r in rows])\\n    X = np.array(\\n        [[float(r[c]) if r[c] not in (\\\"\\\", \\\"nan\\\") else np.nan for c in FEAT_COLS] for r in rows]\\n    )\\n    col_means = np.nanmean(X, axis=0)\\n    for j in range(X.shape[1]):\\n        mask = np.isnan(X[:, j])\\n        X[mask, j] = col_means[j]\\n    return X, depths, doc_ids\\n\\ndef get_oof_predictions(X, y, groups):\\n    gkf = GroupKFold(n_splits=5)\\n    oof_proba = np.zeros(len(y))\\n    fold_aucs = []\\n    for train_idx, test_idx in gkf.split(X, y, groups=groups):\\n        clf = lgb.LGBMClassifier(\\n            n_estimators=200, max_depth=6, learning_rate=0.05,\\n            num_leaves=31, verbose=-1, n_jobs=-1,\\n        )\\n        clf.fit(X[train_idx], y[train_idx])\\n        proba = clf.predict_proba(X[test_idx])[:, 1]\\n        oof_proba[test_idx] = proba\\n        fold_aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[test_idx], proba))\\n    return oof_proba, fold_aucs\\n\\ndef bootstrap_ci(y, oof_proba, doc_ids, n_boot, rng):\\n    unique_docs = np.unique(doc_ids)\\n    n_docs = len(unique_docs)\\n    doc_indices = [np.where(doc_ids == d)[0] for d in unique_docs]\\n    \\n    point_auc = roc_auc_score(y, oof_proba)\\n    boot_aucs = np.empty(n_boot)\\n    \\n    for b in range(n_boot):\\n        sampled = rng.randint(0, n_docs, size=n_docs)\\n        indices = np.concatenate([doc_indices[s] for s in sampled])\\n        try:\\n            boot_aucs[b] = roc_auc_score(y[indices], oof_proba[indices])\\n        except ValueError:\\n            boot_aucs[b] = np.nan\\n    \\n    boot_aucs = boot_aucs[~np.isnan(boot_aucs)]\\n    ci_lower = float(np.percentile(boot_aucs, 2.5))\\n    ci_upper = float(np.percentile(boot_aucs, 97.5))\\n    return point_auc, ci_lower, ci_upper, boot_aucs\\n\\ndef permutation_test(y, oof_proba, doc_ids, n_perm, rng, observed_auc):\\n    unique_docs, inverse = np.unique(doc_ids, return_inverse=True)\\n    n_docs = len(unique_docs)\\n    \\n    count_ge = 0\\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\\n        doc_labels = rng.randint(0, 2, size=n_docs)\\n        perm_y = doc_labels[inverse]\\n        if perm_y.sum() == 0 or perm_y.sum() == len(perm_y):\\n            continue\\n        perm_auc = roc_auc_score(perm_y, oof_proba)\\n        if perm_auc >= observed_auc:\\n            count_ge += 1\\n    return (count_ge + 1) 1)\\n\\ndef main():\\n    t_start = time.time()\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(SEED)\\n    print(\\\"Loading data...\\\", flush=True)\\n    X, depths, doc_ids = load_data()\\n    print(f\\\"  Total samples: {len(depths)}\\\", flush=True)\\n    print(f\\\"  Depth distribution: {dict(zip(*np.unique(depths, return_counts=True)))}\\\", flush=True)\\n    all_pairs = {}\\n    k_star = 0\\n    for k in range(MAX_DEPTH):\\n        d0, d1 = k, k + 1\\n        pair_name = f\\\"{d0}v{d1}\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\", flush=True)\\n        print(f\\\"Pair: {pair_name}\\\", flush=True)\\n        mask = (depths == d0) | (depths == d1)\\n        X_pair = X[mask]\\n        y_pair = (depths[mask] == d1).astype(int)\\n        groups_pair = doc_ids[mask]\\n        print(f\\\"  Samples: {len(y_pair)} (class 0: {(y_pair==0).sum()}, class 1: {(y_pair==1).sum()})\\\", flush=True)\\n        oof_proba, fold_aucs = get_oof_predictions(X_pair, y_pair, groups_pair)\\n        print(f\\\"  Fold AUCs: {[round(a,4) for a in fold_aucs]}\\\", flush=True)\\n        print(f\\\"  Mean AUC: {np.mean(fold_aucs):.4f}\\\", flush=True)\\n        point_auc, ci_lower, ci_upper, boot_aucs = bootstrap_ci(\\n            y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, N_BOOT, rng\\n        )\\n        print(f\\\"  Point AUC (OOF): {point_auc:.4f}\\\", flush=True)\\n        print(f\\\"  95% CI: [{ci_lower:.4f}, {ci_upper:.4f}]\\\", flush=True)\\n        perm_p = permutation_test(y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, N_PERM, rng, point_auc)\\n        print(f\\\"  Permutation p-value: {perm_p:.6f}\\\", flush=True)\\n        ci_lower_above_theta = ci_lower > THETA\\n        if ci_lower_above_theta:\\n            k_star = k + 1\\n        print(f\\\"  CI lower > theta ({THETA}): {ci_lower_above_theta}\\\", flush=True)\\n        all_pairs[pair_name] = {\\n            \\\"auc\\\": round(point_auc, 4),\\n            \\\"auc_mean_folds\\\": round(float(np.mean(fold_aucs)), 4),\\n            \\\"fold_aucs\\\": [round(a, 4) for a in fold_aucs],\\n            \\\"ci_lower\\\": round(ci_lower, 4),\\n            \\\"ci_upper\\\": round(ci_upper, 4),\\n            \\\"ci_lower_above_theta\\\": ci_lower_above_theta,\\n            \\\"perm_p\\\": round(perm_p, 6),\\n            \\\"boot_mean\\\": round(float(np.mean(boot_aucs)), 4),\\n            \\\"boot_std\\\": round(float(np.std(boot_aucs)), 4),\\n        }\\n    total_time = time.time() - t_start\\n    output = {\\n        \\\"experiment\\\": \\\"OLMo-1B C4 continuation, scorer=OLMo-1B (self-scored)\\\",\\n        \\\"method\\\": \\\"GroupKFold(5) OOF + doc-level bootstrap + label permutation\\\",\\n        \\\"n_bootstrap\\\": N_BOOT,\\n        \\\"n_permutation\\\": N_PERM,\\n        \\\"seed\\\": SEED,\\n        \\\"theta\\\": THETA,\\n        \\\"kstar_ci\\\": k_star,\\n        \\\"total_seconds\\\": round(total_time, 1),\\n        \\\"pairs\\\": all_pairs,\\n    }\\n flush=True)\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\"*60, flush=True)\\n    print(\\\"FINAL SUMMARY\\\", flush=True)\\n    print(f\\\"K* (CI-based, theta={THETA}): {k_star}\\\", flush=True)\\n    for {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nMVP Pilot Saturation Study — Complete Pipeline (Steps 2-5)\\n\\nGenerates recursive depth chains from C4 seed data using Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        records.append({\\\"text\\\": truncated, \\\"doc_id\\\": len(records)})\\n        if len(records) >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for r in records:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(records)} seed documents to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n... Use when data already exists via symlinks.\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    data_dir = Path(args.data_dir)\\n    results_dir = Path(args.results_dir)\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    log.info(f\\\"Model: {args.model_path}\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Data dir: {data_dir}\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Samples: {args.num_samples}, Max depth: {args.max_depth}\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Decoding: do_sample={args.do_sample}, top_p={args.top_p}, temperature={args.temperature}\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Generation mode: {args.generation_mode}\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Max new tokens: {args.max_new_tokens}\\\")\\n    scorer_path = args.scorer_path or args.model_path\\n    log.info(f\\\"Scorer: {scorer_path}\\\")\\n\\n    # Load model + tokenizer\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading tokenizer ...\\\")\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n        tokenizer.pad_token_id = tokenizer.eos_token_id\\n    tokenizer.padding_side = \\\"left\\\"\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading model ...\\\")\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.float16,\\n        device_map=\\\"auto\\\",\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n    )\\n    model.eval()\\n    log.info(f\\\"Model loaded on {model.device}\\\")\\n\\n    if args.skip_generation:\\n        log.info(\\\"--skip_generation: skipping Steps 2-3 (data assumed to exist via symlinks)\\\")\\n    else:\\n        # Step 2\\n        log.info(\\\"=\\\" * 40 + \\\" Step 2: Seed Data \\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 40)\\n        prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir, args.num_samples, max_tokens=args.max_new_tokens)\\n\\n        # C4 5K docs, depth 0-4, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B (self-scoring).\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"K_star_lower_bound\\\": 4, \\\"theta\\\": 0.6, \\\"auc_0v1\\\": 0.9838, \\\"auc_1v2\\\": 0.8969, \\\"auc_2v3\\\": 0.81, \\\"auc_3v4\\\": 0.7624, \\\"ci_0v1\\\": [0.982, 0.9855], \\\"ci_1v2\\\": [0.8937, 0.9001], \\\"ci_2v3\\\": [0.8052, 0.8147], \\\"ci_3v4\\\": [0.7543, 0.7706], \\\"ci_method\\\": \\\"5-fold CV\\\", \\\"jsd_0v1\\\": 0.2263, \\\"jsd_1v2\\\": 0.1046, \\\"jsd_2v3\\\": 0.0752, \\\"jsd_3v4\\\": 0.0654}}, \\\"exp_code_qwen1b5\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Code domain (Python) K* measurement: Qwen2.5-1.5B continuation on CodeSearchNet Python subset (Husain et al. 2019). Code 的高度结构化特性（语法约束、缩进、函数签名）应使代际退化慢于 C4 (K*≥2)，可能接近 arXiv (K*=3)。5500 docs, depth 0-5, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"K_star\\\": 2, \\\"theta\\\": 0.6, \\\"auc_0v1\\\": 0.9388, \\\"auc_1v2\\\": 0.7382, \\\"auc_2v3\\\": 0.53, \\\"auc_3v4\\\": 0.5022, \\\"auc_4v5\\\": 0.5219, \\\"ci_0v1\\\": [0.929, 0.949], \\\"ci_1v2\\\": [0.709, 0.767], \\\"ci_2v3\\\": [0.514, 0.546], \\\"jsd_0v1\\\": 0.0814, \\\"jsd_1v2\\\": 0.0443, \\\"jsd_2v3\\\": 0.0038, \\\"ordinal_accuracy\\\": 0.359, \\\"ordinal_mae\\\": 1.204}}, \\\"exp_olmo_retrain_chain\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"OLMo-1B retrain-chain (LoRA rank=16, 1 epoch per generation) on C4 produces K* ≤ 2, confirming prompt-chain as conservative upper bound for K*.\\\"}, \\\"exp_028_subsampling_power\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"C4 K*=2 信号的 minimum sample size: 在 n={1000..5000} 范围内，GroupKFold(5) 1v2 AUC 是否稳定超过 θ=0.60？确定可靠判定 K*=2 所需的最小样本量。\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"n_1000\\\": {\\\"mean_auc\\\": 0.608, \\\"std\\\": 0.012, \\\"detection_prob\\\": 0.9}, \\\"n_2500\\\": {\\\"mean_auc\\\": 0.618, \\\"std\\\": 0.006, \\\"detection_prob\\\": 1}, \\\"n_5000\\\": {\\\"mean_auc\\\": 0.629, \\\"std\\\": 0, \\\"detection_prob\\\": 1}, \\\"key_finding\\\": \\\"n>=2500 gives 100% reliable K*=2 detection. n=1000 already 90%. Signal is robust, not large-sample artifact.\\\"}}, \\\"exp_026_arxiv_filtering\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Graduated depth weighting (α=0.9^d) on arXiv depth chains will outperform both no-filter and binary-cut baselines on downstream benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag), because it preserves useful signal from deeper generations while down-weighting accumulated contamination, rather than discarding data entirely.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"runs_completed\\\": \\\"15/15\\\", \\\"strategies\\\": [\\\"no_filter\\\", \\\"binary\\\", \\\"graduated_a09\\\"], \\\"seeds\\\": [42, 123, 456, 789, 1024], \\\"mmlu\\\": {\\\"no_filter\\\": \\\"0.5899±0.0008\\\", \\\"binary\\\": \\\"0.5876±0.0004\\\", \\\"graduated_a09\\\": \\\"0.5900±0.0011\\\"}, \\\"hellaswag\\\": {\\\"no_filter\\\": \\\"0.6600±0.0002\\\", \\\"binary\\\": \\\"0.6642±0.0004\\\", \\\"graduated_a09\\\": \\\"0.6599±0.0003\\\"}, \\\"delta_vs_no_filter\\\": {\\\"binary_mmlu\\\": \\\"-0.0023\\\", \\\"binary_hellaswag\\\": \\\"+0.0042\\\", \\\"graduated_mmlu\\\": \\\"+0.0001\\\", \\\"graduated_hellaswag\\\": \\\"-0.0001\\\"}, \\\"key_finding\\\": \\\"All three strategies produce nearly identical results. Graduated ≈ no_filter; binary slightly better on HellaSwag (+0.004) but worse on MMLU (-0.002). Hypothesized ordering graduated>binary>no_filter NOT confirmed.\\\"}}, \\\"exp_027_latex_ablation\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"arXiv K*=3 不依赖 LaTeX 格式特征。去除 LaTeX markup 后重新提取 15D features 并跑 pairwise classification，K* 应仍=3，排除 LaTeX 语法作为 confound。\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"k_star\\\": 3, \\\"k_star_preserved\\\": true, \\\"pairwise_auc_original\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.9948, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.8581, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.6436, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.544, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.5299}, \\\"pairwise_auc_stripped\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.9948, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.8297, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.6229, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.5468, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.5476}, \\\"auc_delta\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0, \\\"1v2\\\": -0.028, \\\"2v3\\\": -0.021, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.003, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.018}, \\\"latex_reduction_by_depth\\\": {\\\"depth_0\\\": \\\"0.6%\\\", \\\"depth_5\\\": \\\"4.4%\\\"}, \\\"key_finding\\\": \\\"K*=3 preserved after LaTeX removal. 2v3 AUC 0.6436→0.6229 (still >θ=0.60). LaTeX format is NOT a confound.\\\"}}, \\\"exp_025_pythia_filtering\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Filtering strategy ordering (binary > graduated > no-filter) 在 Pythia-1.4B 上与 Qwen 一致，验证 K*=2 对 curation 策略选择的跨模型预测价值。Pythia-1.4B LoRA rank=8 continued pretraining on C4 depth chain, 3 strategies × 3 seeds, eval MMLU+HellaSwag.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"runs_completed\\\": \\\"9/9\\\", \\\"strategies\\\": [\\\"no_filter\\\", \\\"binary_ours\\\", \\\"graduated_a09\\\"], \\\"seeds\\\": [42, 123, 456], \\\"mmlu\\\": {\\\"no_filter\\\": \\\"0.2568±0.0012\\\", \\\"binary_ours\\\": \\\"0.2585±0.0004\\\", \\\"graduated_a09\\\": \\\"0.2561±0.0009\\\"}, \\\"hellaswag\\\": {\\\"no_filter\\\": \\\"0.5250±0.0008\\\", \\\"binary_ours\\\": \\\"0.5251±0.0001\\\", \\\"graduated_a09\\\": \\\"0.5253±0.0007\\\"}, \\\"delta_vs_no_filter\\\": {\\\"binary_ours_mmlu\\\": \\\"+0.0017\\\", \\\"binary_ours_hellaswag\\\": \\\"+0.0000\\\", \\\"graduated_a09_mmlu\\\": \\\"-0.0007\\\", \\\"graduated_a09_hellaswag\\\": \\\"+0.0003\\\"}, \\\"key_finding\\\": \\\"All three filtering strategies produce nearly identical results on Pythia-1.4B.       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": {\"exp-014\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"OBD depth classifiers trained on synthetic generation-chain data will assign low depth (d0) to most real Common Crawl texts, and predicted AI probability will correlate negatively with vocabulary richness metrics (freq_entropy, TTR), confirming that the 15D feature space captures genuine human-vs-synthetic distributional differences rather than overfitting to synthetic artifacts.\", \"metrics\": {\"superseded_by\": \"exp_014_naturalistic\", \"note\": \"Stale duplicate. All metrics and results are in exp_014_naturalistic.\"}}, \"exp_gemma2b_depth\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Gemma-2-2B (6th architecture family) yields K*=2 on C4 web text under continuation mode, consistent with domain ceiling K*=2 observed across 5 other families (Qwen/Pythia/OLMo/LLaMA/Mistral), strengthening the claim that C4 ceiling is architecture-independent.\", \"metrics\": {\"k_star\": 1, \"pairwise_auc\": {\"0v1\": 0.9534, \"1v2\": 0.5981, \"2v3\": 0.5342, \"3v4\": 0.503, \"4v5\": 0.5026}, \"pairwise_auc_std\": {\"0v1\": 0.003, \"1v2\": 0.008, \"2v3\": 0.013, \"3v4\": 0.003, \"4v5\": 0.013}, \"bootstrap_ci_1v2\": {\"ci_lower\": 0.5882, \"ci_upper\": 0.6076}, \"jsd\": {\"0v1\": 0.09882, \"1v2\": 0.008852, \"2v3\": 0.004904, \"3v4\": 0.003742, \"4v5\": 0.003167}, \"ordinal_mae\": 1.3152, \"ordinal_accuracy\": 0.3321, \"note\": \"K*=1, NOT borderline K*=2. 1v2 AUC=0.5981 < theta=0.60. CI upper=0.6076 crosses theta but point estimate below.\"}}, \"exp_deberta_code_qwen1b5\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"DeBERTa supervised classifier on Code domain yields K*=2 (matching features): v1 K*=2 (1v2=0.676), v2 K*=2 (1v2=0.683)\\n  2. **unified Qwen2.5-1.5B scorer**: mixed K*=2 (1v2=0.7105)，反而高于 confounded (0.7007)\\n  3. **generator label 无增益**: +label K*=2 (1v2=0.7183)，仅 +0.008 AUC\\n- **LLaMA-8B cross-family**: 统一 Qwen scorer 下 LLaMA-8B 单源 K*=1 (1v2=0.6077)，cross-family scoring 更难\\n- **论文策略**: 主表用 unified scorer 结果，appendix 放 vocab-only 作为额外验证\\n\\n### Changepoint K* Framing 指导 (2026-05-20)\\n- **排除 retrain-chain outlier**: 2 个 retrain-chain 实验的 AUC 序列长度和衰减模式与 prompt-chain 完全不同，在 PELT 分析中应排除或单独分析\\n- **Bracket framing**: 论文表述方向为 \\\"K*_CP and K*_θ bracket the discrimination boundary\\\"，不要声称 CP 比 θ 更准\\n- **关键数据**: exact agreement 45.9% (17/37)，within ±1 agreement 94.6% (35/37)，CP 总是 conservative（K*_CP ≤ K*_θ）\\n- **Disagreement AUC range**: [0.607, 0.654]——所有不一致的实验 AUC 都在阈值附近，CP 在 borderline 区域默认保守\\n- **论文用法**: CP 作为 model-free validation，证明 K* 不是 θ=0.60 选择的 artifact\\n\\n### Cron 误判：非 W&B 脚本被标记为 failed (2026-05-19)\\n- **问题**: run_deberta_c4.py 等 DeBERTa 脚本不使用 W&B，训练完成后进程正常退出，但 cron 因\\\"无 W&B metrics\\\"连续 5 次检测失败后 auto-resolve 为 failed\\n- **受影响实验**: exp_32b_c4_6class (westd-42896), exp_deberta_c4_1b5 (cqa1-22115)——均实际成功完成\\n- **修复**: 收到此类 failure 回调时，先 tail_training_logs 查看日志末尾，如有 \\\"SUMMARY\\\" + K* 结果说明实验成功完成\\n- **根治建议**: DeBERTa 脚本应在完成时写一个 sentinel 文件或接入 W&B\\n\\n### Wiki DeBERTa K*=3 — Domain × Classifier 交互发现 (2026-05-19)\\n- **结果**: Wiki DeBERTa K*=3 for 4/5 configs (pythia/qwen7b/llama8b/mistral7b), K*=1 for OLMo\\n- **关键**: DeBERTa 突破了 OBD K*=2 边界——神经表征在 wiki 域上多区分一层深度\\n- **CI 验证**: wiki_pythia 2v3 CI_lower=0.6059>0.60, wiki_qwen7b 2v3=0.6223, wiki_llama8b 2v3=0.6258, wiki_mistral7b 2v3=0.6150\\n- **C4 对比**: C4 DeBERTa K*=2 = OBD K*=2（boundary is intrinsic on C4）\\n- **含义**: K* boundary 在简单域(C4)是内在的(DeBERTa 也突破不了)，在复杂域(wiki)可被更强分类器部分突破\\n- **OLMo 异常**: K*=1, 1v2 AUC=0.518(近随机)——OLMo wiki 生成质量可能有特殊问题\\n\\n### OBD Baseline Bug: wiki_olmo 目录是 wiki_pythia 的复制品 (2026-05-19)\\n- **根因**: `results/exp_wiki_olmo/` 全部 5 个文件 md5 与 `results/exp_wiki_pythia/` 完全相同\\n- OBD pipeline 从未在 wiki_olmo 数据上运行过，目录被整体复制\\n- **修复后 wiki_olmo OBD**: 1v2=0.4983(随机)，与 DeBERTa K*=1 一致\\n- **教训**: 多 config 实验中每个 config 的输出目录必须独立验证，不能假设 pipeline 正确运行了所有 config\\n\\n### Code Cross-Scorer: K*=2 Scorer-Independent (2026-05-19)\\n- **exp_code_qwen1b5_pythia_scorer**: Pythia-1.4B scorer 下 Qwen Code 文本 K*=2（与 Qwen self-scorer 一致）\\n- 1v2 AUC: 0.7197 (Pythia) vs 0.7382 (Qwen)，Δ=-0.019（Pythia 特征提取稍弱但远超 θ=0.60）\\n- 2v3: 0.5308 vs 0.5300，几乎无变化\\n- **结论**: D060 Wikipedia scorer confound 不影响 Code domain（Code 1v2 AUC 远离阈值）\\n- **Scorer confound 影响规律**: 仅在 AUC 极接近 θ 时翻转 K*（Wikipedia 1v2~0.59-0.61），AUC 远离 θ 时 scorer 选择不影响 K* 判定\\n\\n### M011: 实验启动必须 nvidia-smi 验证 (2026-05-19)\\n- **问题**: exp_code_qwen7b 3 次报告\\\"已启动\\\"但实际未运行，cuda:1 空转 9 小时\\n- **根因**: (1) cqa1 无法访问 huggingface.co，datasets.load_dataset 触发 httpx client closed (2) 提交后未验证 GPU 实际占用\\n- **硬规则**: 所有实验提交后必须立即 `nvidia-smi` 确认目标 GPU 有进程 + 显存占用 > 0。如果 0% util / 0 MiB，说明没启动成功，不要回报\\\"已启动\\\"\\n- **修复 pattern**: cqa1 需要 HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com 环境变量；数据文件直接复制而非下载\\n\\n### M012: submit_training_job 必须 bash -c 包裹 conda (2026-05-20)\\n- **问题**: Code DeBERTa 连续 2 次 exit 127 (python not found)\\n- **根因**: tmux 用 sh 执行命令，`source` 和 `conda activate` 是 bash built-in，sh 下不工作\\n- **正确命令**: `bash -c \\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python ...\\\"`\\n- **错误命令**: 直接 `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 python ...`（没有激活 conda，python 不在 PATH）\\n\\n### Pythia Code K*=1 — 跨模型一致性部分否定 (2026-05-19)\\n- **结果**: Pythia-1.4B code domain K*=1 (1v2 AUC=0.5983, 0.17pp below θ=0.60)\\n- 3/5 folds ≥ θ (0.601, 0.600, 0.602)，但 mean 未达标\\n- Qwen Code K*=2 (AUC=0.7382) vs Pythia K*=1 → code K* 不完全 domain-governed\\n- **解释**: Pythia 的 code 能力弱（The Pile 训练，code 比例低），第一代即严重退化\\n- **Framing 影响**: \\\"domain sets ceiling, model competency determines if ceiling is reached\\\"\\n\\n### Retrain-Chain K*=7 跨模型确认 (2026-05-19)\\n- **Qwen2.5-1.5B**: K*≥7, 6v7 AUC=0.641 [0.633, 0.649]\\n- **Pythia-1.4B**: K*=7, 6v7 AUC=0.634 [0.628, 0.641]\\n- 两个架构（GPT-NeoX vs Qwen）retrain K* 完全一致\\n- AUC 衰减形态相似：~0.98 → ~0.63-0.64, 6v7 收敛到相同水平\\n- Pythia 中间 depth AUC 稍高（1v2: 0.947 vs 0.900），可能因更小模型每代漂移更大\\n- **论文 claim**: \\\"retrain K* 由 protocol (LoRA fine-tune on own output) 决定，不由模型架构决定\\\"\\n- **文献支撑**: Bertrand et al. (ICLR 2024) 的理论框架解释了跨模型收敛性\\n\\n### Bootstrap CI 数据泄漏教训 (2026-05-18)\\n- **问题**: retrain-chain 的 bootstrap CI 偏高 (+6.4pp on 1v2)，原因是 bootstrap resampling 未保持 GroupKFold 结构\\n- 重采样打破了 doc-level grouping，导致同一 doc 的不同 depth 出现在 train 和 test 中 → 数据泄漏\\n- **解决**: 废弃 bootstrap CI，使用 fold-based CI (mean ± 1.96 * std / sqrt(n_folds))\\n- **Path B 决策**: 只改 retrain-chain 的 CI 为 fold-based，主 pipeline bootstrap 保留（已验证正确）\\n\\n### Power Curve: n≥2000 Suffices (2026-05-18)\\n- exp_028_power_curve: n=2000 → 100% K*=2 detection, n=1000 → borderline (30-90% depending on seed)\\n- **论文策略 (Director 决策 C)**: 只报 \\\"n≥2000 suffices, we use n=5000\\\"，appendix 放 power_curve.png\\n- 两个 power curve 实验 (exp_028_power_curve vs exp_028_subsampling_power) 在 n=1000 处差 60pp，根因是随机种子不同 + AUC 恰好在 θ 边界。方法学完全一致，10 次 resample 在阈值附近 detection rate 估计本身不稳定\\n- **教训**: 阈值附近的 detection rate 需要 ≥50 resamples 才稳定；远离阈值的 rate 10 次即可\\n\\n### Code Domain (Python) K*=2 — 第 4 域确认 Domain-Governed K* (2026-05-18)\\n- **数据源**: CodeSearchNet Python subset (Husain et al. 2019), 5500 docs, avg 711 chars\\n- **结果 (Qwen2.5-1.5B)**: K*=2, 1v2 AUC=0.7382 [0.709, 0.767], 2v3 AUC=0.5300 [0.514, 0.546]\\n- 1v2 AUC 高于多数 C4 模型 (0.61-0.70)，但 2v3 骤降（JSD 0.044→0.004，10x 衰减）\\n- **4 域全景**: arXiv(3) > C4(2) = Code(2) > Wikipedia(1)\\n- Code 与 C4 共享 K*=2 dominant mode，虽然代码有语法约束但模式重复导致快速坍塌\\n- **注意**: The Stack v2 为 gated dataset，用了开放的 CodeSearchNet 替代。论文入稿须引用 CodeSearchNet\\n- **Bug M010**: run_pipeline.py:462 `int(doc_id)` 对 string ID 崩溃，已 patched\\n\\n### Qwen Retrain-Chain K*≥4 — 第 2 个架构家族确认 (2026-05-18)\\n- Qwen2.5-1.5B LoRA rank=16, C4 5K docs, depth 0-4, self-scoring\\n- **AUC (fold-based)**: 0v1=0.9838, 1v2=0.8969, 2v3=0.8100, 3v4=0.7624 (all >> θ=0.60)\\n- **CI (5-fold CV)**: 0v1=[0.982, 0.986], 1v2=[0.894, 0.900], 2v3=[0.805, 0.815], 3v4=[0.754, 0.771]\\n- **JSD**: 0v1=0.2263, 1v2=0.1046, 2v3=0.0752, 3v4=0.0654\\n- 与 Pythia retrain-chain (也是 K*≥4) 一致，2/2 架构家族确认\\n- **论文意义**: prompt-chain K*=2 低估真实代际可区分性，retrain-chain 在不同 arch 下均显示 K*≥4\\n- Depth extension (depth 5-7) 在进行中，目标找到真实 K* ceiling\\n\\n### OLMo 在 cqa1 持续下载失败 (2026-05-18)\\n- M009: 首次下载权重中断\\n- 重试: 网络端口耗尽 (pip 安装占用连接), hf-mirror 307 重定向失败\\n- **决定**: Director 放弃 OLMo retrain-chain，cuda:0 改跑 Qwen retrain-chain\\n- ModelScope 未安装在 cqa1，无法作替代\\n\\n### Wikipedia Scorer Confound Confirmed (2026-05-17)\\n- **发现**: Qwen-7B Wikipedia K*=2 是 Qwen-1.5B scorer 的 family bias artifact\\n- Pythia-1.4B scorer 下: 1v2 AUC=0.5870 (< θ=0.60) → K*=1\\n- Qwen-1.5B scorer 下: 1v2 AUC=0.6108 (barely > θ=0.60) → K*=2\\n- **机制**: Qwen scorer 对 Qwen 生成文本有 uniform +0.005~0.027 AUC inflation (family bias)\\n- **影响**: Wikipedia 可能统一为 K*=1（所有模型），等 exp_wiki_scorer_confound_1b5 确认\\n- C4/arXiv 不受影响: C4 已有 cross-scorer 验证(exp_022)；arXiv AUC 远超阈值\\n- **论文调整**: \\\"Qwen uniqueness on Wikipedia\\\" 需改为 \\\"scorer confound caveat\\\"，但核心 story (domain-dependent K*) 反而更 clean: arXiv(3)>C4(2)>Wikipedia(1)\\n\\n### Scorer Confound 预案 — 14B/32B 实验 (2026-05-17)\\n- **触发条件**: exp_14b_c4 或 exp_32b_c4 的 K*=1 时\\n- **操作**: 用 Qwen2.5-14B 作为 scorer（替代 1.5B）重新计算 surprisal features → 重跑 pairwise\\n\\n### cqa1 网络环境 (2026-05-19)\\n- cqa1 无法直连   "content": "# Unified 10K bootstrap CI recomputation for Table 2 borderline configurations.\n# Addresses R14-W1: confirms K* verdicts are stable under 10K vs 1K resamples.\n# Pure CPU computation on OOF predictions — no model inference needed.\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport lightgbm as lgb\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import GroupKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n\nFEAT_COLS = [\n    \"surp_mean\", \"surp_std\", \"surp_skew\", \"surp_kurt\",\n    \"surp_d1_mean\", \"surp_d1_std\", \"surp_d1_skew\",\n    \"surp_d2_mean\", \"surp_d2_std\",\n    \"ttr\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"self_bleu\",\n    \"freq_kurtosis\", \"freq_entropy\", \"low_freq_ratio\",\n]\n\nTHETA = 0.60\nSEED = 42\n\nCASES = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"LLaMA-3.1-8B (exp_018)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_018_llama8b\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_lower_gt_theta\",\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"Gemma-2-2B (exp_gemma2b)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_gemma2b_depth\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_upper_lt_theta\",\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"Mistral-7B (exp_019)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data_exp019_mistral7b\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_upper_lt_theta\",\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"512-tok (exp_021)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_021_length_512\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_lower_gt_theta\",\n    },\n]\n\nOUT_DIR = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/results/exp_bootstrap_10k\")\n\n\ndef load_data(data_dir):\n    with open(Path(data_dir) / \"features.csv\") as f:\n        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))\n    depths = np.array([int(r[\"depth\"]) for r in rows])\n    doc_ids = np.array([int(r[\"doc_id\"]) for r in rows])\n    X = np.array(\n        [[float(r[c]) if r[c] not in (\"\", \"nan\") else np.nan for c in FEAT_COLS] for r in rows]\n    )\n    col_means = np.nanmean(X, axis=0)\n    for j in range(X.shape[1]):\n        mask = np.isnan(X[:, j])\n        X[mask, j] = col_means[j]\n    return X, depths, doc_ids\n\n\ndef get_oof_predictions(X, y, groups):\n    gkf = GroupKFold(n_splits=5)\n    oof_proba = np.zeros(len(y))\n    fold_aucs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in gkf.split(X, y, groups=groups):\n        clf = lgb.LGBMClassifier(\n            n_estimators=200, max_depth=6, learning_rate=0.05,\n            num_leaves=31, verbose=-1, n_jobs=-1,\n        )\n        clf.fit(X[train_idx], y[train_idx])\n        proba = clf.predict_proba(X[test_idx])[:, 1]\n        oof_proba[test_idx] = proba\n        fold_aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[test_idx], proba))\n    return oof_proba, fold_aucs\n\n\ndef bootstrap_ci(y, oof_proba, doc_ids, n_boot, rng):\n    unique_docs = np.unique(doc_ids)\n    n_docs = len(unique_docs)\n    doc_indices = [np.where(doc_ids == d)[0] for d in unique_docs]\n\n    point_auc = roc_auc_score(y, oof_proba)\n    boot_aucs = np.empty(n_boot)\n\n    for b in range(n_boot):\n        sampled = rng.randint(0, n_docs, size=n_docs)\n        indices = np.concatenate([doc_indices[s] for s in sampled])\n        try:\n            boot_aucs[b] = roc_auc_score(y[indices], oof_proba[indices])\n        except ValueError:\n            boot_aucs[b] = np.nan\n\n    boot_aucs = boot_aucs[~np.isnan(boot_aucs)]\n    ci_lower = float(np.percentile(boot_aucs, 2.5))\n    ci_upper = float(np.percentile(boot_aucs, 97.5))\n    return point_auc, ci_lower, ci_upper, boot_aucs\n\n\ndef permutation_test(y, oof_proba, doc_ids, n_perm, rng, observed_auc):\n    unique_docs, inverse = np.unique(doc_ids, return_inverse=True)\n    n_docs = len(unique_docs)\n\n    count_ge = 0\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\n        doc_labels = rng.randint(0, 2, size=n_docs)\n        perm_y = doc_labels[inverse]\n        if perm_y.sum() == 0 or perm_y.sum() == len(perm_y):\n            continue\n        perm_auc = roc_auc_score(perm_y, oof_proba)\n        if perm_auc >= observed_auc:\n            count_ge += 1\n    return (count_ge + 1) / (n_perm + 1)\n\n\ndef verdict(check, ci_lower, ci_upper, point_auc):\n    if check == \"ci_lower_gt_theta\":\n        if ci_lower > THETA:\n            return \"K*=2\"\n        elif point_auc > THETA:\n            return \"BORDERLINE\"\n        else:\n            return \"K*=1\"\n    elif check == \"ci_upper_lt_theta\":\n        if ci_upper < THETA:\n            return \"K*=1\"\n        elif point_auc < THETA:\n            return \"BORDERLINE\"\n        else:\n            return \"K*=2\"\n    return \"UNKNOWN\"\n\n\ndef main():\n    t_start = time.time()\n    all_results = {}\n\n    for case in CASES:\n        name = case[\"name\"]\n        d0, d1 = case[\"depth_pair\"]\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"Case: {name} (depth {d0}v{d1})\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n        t0 = time.time()\n        X, depths, doc_ids = load_data(case[\"data_dir\"])\n\n        mask = (depths == d0) | (depths == d1)\n        X_pair = X[mask]\n        y_pair = (depths[mask] == d1).astype(int)\n        groups_pair = doc_ids[mask]\n\n        print(f\"  Samples: {len(y_pair)} (class 0: {(y_pair==0).sum()}, class 1: {(y_pair==1).sum()})\", flush=True)\n\n        oof_proba, fold_aucs = get_oof_predictions(X_pair, y_pair, groups_pair)\n        print(f\"  Fold AUCs: {[round(a,4) for a in fold_aucs]}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"  Mean AUC: {np.mean(fold_aucs):.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n        # 1K bootstrap\n        rng_1k = np.random.RandomState(SEED)\n        point_auc_1k, ci_lower_1k, ci_upper_1k, boot_aucs_1k = bootstrap_ci(\n            y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, 1000, rng_1k\n        )\n        v_1k = verdict(case[\"check\"], ci_lower_1k, ci_upper_1k, point_auc_1k)\n        print(f\"  1K  bootstrap: AUC={point_auc_1k:.4f}  CI=[{ci_lower_1k:.4f}, {ci_upper_1k:.4f}]  -> {v_1k}\", flush=True)\n\n        # 10K bootstrap (fresh RNG to avoid seed-chain dependency)\n        rng_10k = np.random.RandomState(SEED + 1)\n        point_auc_10k, ci_lower_10k, ci_upper_10k, boot_aucs_10k = bootstrap_ci(\n            y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, 10000, rng_10k\n        )\n        v_10k = verdict(case[\"check\"], ci_lower_10k, ci_upper_10k, point_auc_10k)\n        print(f\"  10K bootstrap: AUC={point_auc_10k:.4f}  CI=[{ci_lower_10k:.4f}, {ci_upper_10k:.4f}]  -> {v_10k}\", flush=True)\n\n        # 10K permutation test\n        rng_perm = np.random.RandomState(SEED + 2)\n        perm_p = permutation_test(y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, 10000, rng_perm, point_auc_10k)\n        print(f\"  Permutation p-value (10K): {perm_p:.6f}\", flush=True)\n\n        flipped = v_1k != v_10k\n        if flipped:\n            print(f\"  *** VERDICT FLIPPED: {v_1k} -> {v_10k} ***\", flush=True)\n        else:\n            print(f\"  Verdict stable: {v_10k}\", flush=True)\n\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        print(f\"  Done in {elapsed:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n        all_results[name] = {\n            \"depth_pair\": f\"{d0}v{d1}\",\n            \"1v2_auc\": round(point_auc_10k, 4),\n            \"fold_aucs\": [round(a, 4) for a in fold_aucs],\n            \"ci_1k\": [round(ci_lower_1k, 4), round(ci_upper_1k, 4)],\n            \"ci_10k\": [round(ci_lower_10k, 4), round(ci_upper_10k, 4)],\n            \"ci_width_1k\": round(ci_upper_1k - ci_lower_1k, 4),\n            \"ci_width_10k\": round(ci_upper_10k - ci_lower_10k, 4),\n            \"perm_p_10k\": round(perm_p, 6),\n            \"boot_mean_10k\": round(float(np.mean(boot_aucs_10k)), 4),\n            \"boot_std_10k\": round(float(np.std(boot_aucs_10k)), 4),\n            \"verdict_1k\": v_1k,\n            \"verdict_10k\": v_10k,\n            \"flipped\": flipped,\n        }\n\n    total_time = time.time() - t_start\n\n    any_flipped = any(r[\"flipped\"] for r in all_results.values())\n\n    output = {\n        \"description\": \"Table 2 borderline bootstrap CI: 1K vs 10K comparison\",\n        \"method\": \"GroupKFold(5) OOF + doc-level bootstrap + label permutation\",\n        \"theta\": THETA,\n        \"seed\": SEED,\n        \"total_seconds\": round(total_time, 1),\n        \"any_verdict_flipped\": any_flipped,\n        \"note\": \"exp_017 (Qwen-cont) and exp_002 (OLMo-1B) excluded: features.csv not found on disk\",\n        \"cases\": all_results,\n    }\n\n    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path = OUT_DIR / \"bootstrap_10k_comparison.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60, flush=True)\n    print(\"FINAL SUMMARY\", flush=True)\n    print(\"=\"*60, flush=True)\n    print(f\"Theta = {THETA}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Any verdict flipped: {any_flipped}\", flush=True)\n    for name, r in all_results.items():\n        flip_flag = \" *** FLIPPED ***\" if r[\"flipped\"] else \"\"\n        print(f\"  {name}:\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"    AUC={r['1v2_auc']:.4f}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"    1K  CI=[{r['ci_1k'][0]:.4f}, {r['ci_1k'][1]:.4f}] width={r['ci_width_1k']:.4f} -> {r['verdict_1k']}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"    10K CI=[{r['ci_10k'][0]:.4f}, {r['ci_10k'][1]:.4f}] width={r['ci_width_10k']:.4f} -> {r['verdict_10k']}{flip_flag}\", flush=True)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal time: {total_time:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"

GroupKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n\nFEAT_COLS = [\n    \"surp_mean\", \"surp_std\", \"surp_skew\", \"surp_kurt\",\n    \"surp_d1_mean\", \"surp_d1_std\", \"surp_d1_skew\",\n    \"surp_d2_mean\", \"surp_d2_std\",\n    \"ttr\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"self_bleu\",\n    \"freq_kurtosis\", \"freq_entropy\", \"low_freq_ratio\",\n]\n\nTHETA = 0.60\nSEED = 42\n\nCASES = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"LLaMA-3.1-8B (exp_018)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_018_llama8b\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_lower_gt_theta\",\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"Gemma-2-2B (exp_gemma2b)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_gemma2b_depth\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_upper_lt_theta\",\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"Mistral-7B (exp_019)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data_exp019_mistral7b\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_upper_lt_theta\",\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"512-tok (exp_021)\",\n        \"data_dir\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_021_length_512\",\n        \"depth_pair\": (1, 2),\n        \"check\": \"ci_lower_gt_theta\",\n    },\n]\n\nOUT_DIR = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/results/exp_bootstrap_10k\")\n\n\ndef load_data(data_dir):\n    with open(Path(data_dir) / \"features.csv\") as f:\n        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))\n    depths = np.array([int(r[\"depth\"]) for r in rows])\n    doc_ids = np.array([int(r[\"doc_id\"]) for r in rows])\n    X = np.array(\n        [[float(r[c]) if r[c] not in (\"\", \"nan\") else np.nan for c in FEAT_COLS] for r in rows]\n    )\n    col_means = np.nanmean(X, axis=0)\n    for j in range(X.shape[1]):\n        mask = np.isnan(X[:, j])\n        X[mask, j] = col_means[j]\n    return X, depths, doc_ids\n\n\ndef get_oof_predictions(X, y, groups):\n    gkf = GroupKFold(n_splits=5)\n    oof_proba = np.zeros(len(y))\n    fold_aucs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in gkf.split(X, y, groups=groups):\n        clf = lgb.LGBMClassifier(\n            n_estimators=200, max_depth=6, learning_rate=0.05,\n            num_leaves=31, verbose=-1, n_jobs=-1,\n        )\n        clf.fit(X[train_idx], y[train_idx])\n        proba = clf.predict_proba(X[test_idx])[:, 1]\n        oof_proba[test_idx] = proba\n        fold_aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[test_idx], proba))\n    return oof_proba, fold_aucs\n\n\ndef bootstrap_ci(y, oof_proba, doc_ids, n_boot, rng):\n    unique_docs = np.unique(doc_ids)\n    n_docs = len(unique_docs)\n    doc_indices = [np.where(doc_ids == d)[0] for d in unique_docs]\n\n    point_auc = roc_auc_score(y, oof_proba)\n    boot_aucs = np.empty(n_boot)\n\n    for b in range(n_boot):\n        sampled = rng.randint(0, n_docs, size=n_docs)\n        indices = np.concatenate([doc_indices[s] for s in sampled])\n        try:\n            boot_aucs[b] = roc_auc_score(y[indices], oof_proba[indices])\n        except ValueError:\n            boot_aucs[b] = np.nan\n\n    boot_aucs = boot_aucs[~np.isnan(boot_aucs)]\n    ci_lower = float(np.percentile(boot_aucs, 2.5))\n    ci_upper = float(np.percentile(boot_aucs, 97.5))\n    return point_auc, ci_lower, ci_upper, boot_aucs\n\n\ndef permutation_test(y, oof_proba, doc_ids, n_perm, rng, observed_auc):\n    unique_docs, inverse = np.unique(doc_ids, return_inverse=True)\n    n_docs = len(unique_docs)\n\n    count_ge = 0\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\n        doc_labels = rng.randint(0, 2, size=n_docs)\n        perm_y = doc_labels[inverse]\n        if perm_y.sum() == 0 or perm_y.sum() == len(perm_y):\n            continue\n        perm_auc = roc_auc_score(perm_y, oof_proba)\n        if perm_auc >= observed_auc:\n            count_ge += 1\n    return (count_ge + 1) / (n_perm + 1)\n\n\ndef verdict(check, ci_lower, ci_upper, point_auc):\n    if check == \"ci_lower_gt_theta\":\n        if ci_lower > THETA:\n            return \"K*=2\"\n        elif point_auc > THETA:\n            return \"BORDERLINE\"\n        else:\n            return \"K*=1\"\n    elif check == \"ci_upper_lt_theta\":\n        if ci_upper < THETA:\n            return \"K*=1\"\n        elif point_auc < THETA:\n            return \"BORDERLINE\"\n        else:\n            return \"K*=2\"\n    return \"UNKNOWN\"\n\n\ndef main():\n    t_start = time.time()\n    all_results = {}\n\n    for case in CASES:\n        name = case[\"name\"]\n        d0, d1 = case[\"depth_pair\"]\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"Case: {name} (depth {d0}v{d1})\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n        t0 = time.time()\n        X, depths, doc_ids = load_data(case[\"data_dir\"])\n\n        mask = (depths == d0) | (depths == d1)\n        X_pair = X[mask]\n        y_pair = (depths[mask] == d1).astype(int)\n        groups_pair = doc_ids[mask]\n\n        print(f\"  Samples: {len(y_pair)} (class 0: {(y_pair==0).sum()}, class 1: {(y_pair==1).sum()})\", flush=True)\n\n        oof_proba, fold_aucs = get_oof_predictions(X_pair, y_pair, groups_pair)\n        print(f\"  Fold AUCs: {[round(a,4) for a in fold_aucs]}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"  Mean AUC: {np.mean(fold_aucs):.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n        # 1K bootstrap\n        rng_1k = np.random.RandomState(SEED)\n        point_auc_1k, ci_lower_1k, ci_upper_1k, boot_aucs_1k = bootstrap_ci(\n            y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, 1000, rng_1k\n        )\n        v_1k = verdict(case[\"check\"], ci_lower_1k, ci_upper_1k, point_auc_1k)\n        print(f\"  1K  bootstrap: AUC={point_auc_1k:.4f}  CI=[{ci_lower_1k:.4f}, {ci_upper_1k:.4f}]  -> {v_1k}\", flush=True)\n\n        # 10K bootstrap (fresh RNG to avoid seed-chain dependency)\n        rng_10k = np.random.RandomState(SEED + 1)\n        point_auc_10k, ci_lower_10k, ci_upper_10k, boot_aucs_10k = bootstrap_ci(\n            y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, 10000, rng_10k\n        )\n        v_10k = verdict(case[\"check\"], ci_lower_10k, ci_upper_10k, point_auc_10k)\n        print(f\"  10K bootstrap: AUC={point_auc_10k:.4f}  CI=[{ci_lower_10k:.4f}, {ci_upper_10k:.4f}]  -> {v_10k}\", flush=True)\n\n        # 10K permutation test\n        rng_perm = np.random.RandomState(SEED + 2)\n        perm_p = permutation_test(y_pair, oof_proba, groups_pair, 10000, rng_perm, point_auc_10k)\n        print(f\"  Permutation p-value (10K): {perm_p:.6f}\", flush=True)\n\n        flipped = v_1k != v_10k\n        if flipped:\n            print(f\"  *** VERDICT FLIPPED: {v_1k} -> {v_10k} ***\", flush=True)\n        else:\n            print(f\"  Verdict stable: {v_10k}\", flush=True)\n\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        print(f\"  Done in {elapsed:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n        all_results[name] = {\n            \"depth_pair\": f\"{d0}v{d1}\",\n            \"1v2_auc\": round(point_auc_10k, 4),\n            \"fold_aucs\": [round(a, 4) for a in fold_aucs],\n            \"ci_1k\": [round(ci_lower_1k, 4), round(ci_upper_1k, 4)],\n            \"ci_10k\": [round(ci_lower_10k, 4), round(ci_upper_10k, 4)],\n            \"ci_width_1k\": round(ci_upper_1k - ci_lower_1k, 4),\n            \"ci_width_10k\": round(ci_upper_10k - ci_lower_10k, 4),\n            \"perm_p_10k\": round(perm_p, 6),\n            \"boot_mean_10k\": round(float(np.mean(boot_aucs_10k)), 4),\n            \"boot_std_10k\": round(float(np.std(boot_aucs_10k)), 4),\n            \"verdict_1k\": v_1k,\n            \"verdict_10k\": v_10k,\n            \"flipped\": flipped,\n        }\n\n    total_time = time.time() - t_start\n\n    any_flipped = any(r[\"flipped\"] for r in all_results.values())\n\n    output = {\n        \"description\": \"Table 2 borderline bootstrap CI: 1K vs 10K comparison\",\n        \"method\": \"GroupKFold(5) OOF + doc-level bootstrap + label permutation\",\n        \"theta\": THETA,\n        \"seed\": SEED,\n        \"total_seconds\": round(total_time, 1),\n        \"any_verdict_flipped\": any_flipped,\n        \"note\": \"exp_017 (Qwen-cont) and exp_002 (OLMo-1B) excluded: features.csv not found on disk\",\n        \"cases\": all_results,\n    }\n\n    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path = OUT_DIR / \"bootstrap_10k_comparison.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60, flush=True)\n    print(\"FINAL SUMMARY\", flush=True)\n    print(\"=\"*60, flush=True)\n    print(f\"Theta = {THETA}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Any verdict flipped: {any_flipped}\", flush=True)\n    for name, r in all_results.items():\n        flip_flag = \" *** FLIPPED ***\" if r[\"flipped\"] else \"\"\n        print(f\"  {name}:\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"    AUC={r['1v2_auc']:.4f}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"    1K  CI=[{r['ci_1k'][0]:.4f}, {r['ci_1k'][1]:.4f}] width={r['ci_width_1k']:.4f} -> {r['verdict_1k']}\", flush=True)\n        print(f\"    10K CI=[{r['ci_10k'][0]:.4f}, {r['ci_10k'][1]:.4f}] width={r['ci_width_10k']:.4f} -> {r['verdict_10k']}{flip_flag}\", flush=True)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal time: {total_time:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",

CI=[{r['ci_10k'][0]:.4f}, {r['ci_10k'][1]:.4f}] width={r['ci_width_10k']:.4f} -> {r['verdict_10k']}{flip_flag}\", flush=True)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal time: {total_time:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"Script uploaded\"",
  "account": "root"
}
